EN_LORE: A yew bow with the string drawn none too tight. Reliable enough all the same. Both limbs take the load evenly and release with a clean snap.

VARIANT_ID: refined
SOURCE_TYPES: CRAFTING, ENTITY_DROP, FISHING, TRADING, VAULT

EN_LORE: A yew bow with the string drawn none too tight. Reliable enough all the same. Springy limbs and a straight string path send the shot away with little wasted vibration.

VARIANT_ID: damaged

ZH_LORE: 纯金的头盔，戴上很显眼，防护很有限。
EN_LORE: A helmet of solid gold. Very noticeable, and not very protective.

VARIANT_ID: damaged
SOURCE_TYPES: CHEST_LOOT, ENTITY_DROP
WEIGHTS: CHEST_LOOT=70.0, ENTITY_DROP=80.0
ZH_NAME: 磨损的金头盔
EN_NAME: Worn Golden Helmet
ZH_LORE: 纯金的头盔，戴上很显眼，防护很有限。 承受过重击的地方已经凹下去，接缝还撑着，不能再太依赖它。
EN_LORE: A helmet of solid gold. Very noticeable, and not very protective. Heavy blows have left dents. The seams still hold, though little strength remains in them.

VARIANT_ID: standard
SOURCE_TYPES: CHEST_LOOT, CRAFTING, ENTITY_DROP
WEIGHTS: CHEST_LOOT=25.0, CRAFTING=13.0, ENTITY_DROP=18.0
ZH_NAME: 工整的金头盔
EN_NAME: Well-made Golden Helmet
ZH_LORE: 纯金的头盔，戴上很显眼，防护很有限。 边缘磨平，接缝牢靠，活动时没有多余的摩擦声。
EN_LORE: A helmet of solid gold. Very noticeable, and not very protective. Smooth edges and sound seams move together without an unwanted scrape.

VARIANT_ID: refined
SOURCE_TYPES: CHEST_LOOT, CRAFTING, ENTITY_DROP
WEIGHTS: CHEST_LOOT=5.0, CRAFTING=2.0, ENTITY_DROP=2.0
ZH_NAME: 精制的金头盔
EN_NAME: Refined Golden Helmet
ZH_LORE: 纯金的头盔，戴上很显眼，防护很有限。 受力处加固得恰到好处，内侧也仔细打磨过，贴身而不硌人。
EN_LORE: A helmet of solid gold. Very noticeable, and not very protective. Reinforcement follows the lines of strain, with the inside finished smooth against the wearer.

VARIANT_ID: handmade
SOURCE_TYPES: CRAFTING
WEIGHTS: CRAFTING=85.0
ZH_NAME: 手工打制的金头盔
EN_NAME: Handmade Golden Helmet
ZH_LORE: 纯金的头盔，戴上很显眼，防护很有限。 接缝收得仓促，几个活动的位置略显僵硬，勉强能护住要害。
EN_LORE: A helmet of solid gold. Very noticeable, and not very protective. Hasty seams bind at the joints; it covers the vital parts, though movement is awkward.

=== ITEM_ID: minecraft:golden_hoe ===
BASE_DESCRIPTION
ZH_LORE: 金锄头，农田里最显眼也最短命的家伙。
EN_LORE: A golden hoe, the most conspicuous and shortest-lived implement in any field.

VARIANT_ID: damaged
SOURCE_TYPES: CHEST_LOOT
WEIGHTS: CHEST_LOOT=70.0
ZH_NAME: 磨损的金锄
EN_NAME: Worn Golden Hoe
ZH_LORE: 金锄头，农田里最显眼也最短命的家伙。 常受力的边缘已经磨圆，柄上几道裂纹让人不敢使尽力气。
EN_LORE: A golden hoe, the most conspicuous and shortest-lived implement in any field. The working edge has rounded off, and cracks in the handle discourage a hard grip.

VARIANT_ID: standard
SOURCE_TYPES: CHEST_LOOT, CRAFTING
WEIGHTS: CHEST_LOOT=25.0, CRAFTING=13.0
ZH_NAME: 工整的金锄
EN_NAME: Well-made Golden Hoe
ZH_LORE: 金锄头，农田里最显眼也最短命的家伙。 工作端打磨得当，握柄扎实，做起常用的活计相当顺手。
EN_LORE: A golden hoe, the most conspicuous and shortest-lived implement in any field. A properly finished working end and a sound handle suit steady everyday work.

VARIANT_ID: refined
SOURCE_TYPES: CHEST_LOOT, CRAFTING
WEIGHTS: CHEST_LOOT=5.0, CRAFTING=2.0
ZH_NAME: 精制的金锄
EN_NAME: Refined Golden Hoe
ZH_LORE: 金锄头，农田里最显眼也最短命的家伙。 工作端的角度修得很准，握柄也配得合宜，力气几乎不会白费。
EN_LORE: A golden hoe, the most conspicuous and shortest-lived implement in any field. An accurately shaped working end and a fitted handle put nearly every effort to use.

VARIANT_ID: handmade
SOURCE_TYPES: CRAFTING
WEIGHTS: CRAFTING=85.0
ZH_NAME: 手工打制的金锄
EN_NAME: Handmade Golden Hoe
ZH_LORE: 金锄头，农田里最显眼也最短命的家伙。 柄与工作端接得略偏，边缘也没有细磨，干活时得多费些力气。
EN_LORE: A golden hoe, the most conspicuous and shortest-lived implement in any field. The working end sits slightly off the handle and lacks a fine finish, making each task harder.

=== ITEM_ID: minecraft:poisonous_potato ===
BASE_DESCRIPTION
ZH_LORE: 绿斑透过薯皮浮出来，切面也不干净，不该当成普通土豆。
EN_LORE: Green patches show through the skin and spoil the cut, a poor substitute for an ordinary potato.

VARIANT_ID: stale
SOURCE_TYPES: BLOCK_DROP, CHEST_LOOT
WEIGHTS: BLOCK_DROP=65.0, CHEST_LOOT=75.0
ZH_NAME: 放陈的毒马铃薯
EN_NAME: Stale Poisonous Potato
ZH_LORE: 绿斑透过薯皮浮出来，切面也不干净，不该当成普通土豆。 颜色已经深得不自然，气味也更浓烈，吃下去恐怕要付出些代价。
EN_LORE: Green patches show through the skin and spoil the cut, a poor substitute for an ordinary potato. Its colour has deepened unnaturally and its smell grown stronger; swallowing it promises a cost.

VARIANT_ID: ordinary
SOURCE_TYPES: BLOCK_DROP, CHEST_LOOT
WEIGHTS: BLOCK_DROP=32.0, CHEST_LOOT=23.0
ZH_NAME: 寻常的毒马铃薯
EN_NAME: Plain Poisonous Potato
ZH_LORE: 绿斑透过薯皮浮出来，切面也不干净，不该当成普通土豆。 还能辨出原来的形状，但那股刺鼻的味道说明它本就不是好食材。
EN_LORE: Green patches show through the skin and spoil the cut, a poor substitute for an ordinary potato. Its original shape remains, though the sharp smell says it was never a good ingredient.

VARIANT_ID: choice
SOURCE_TYPES: BLOCK_DROP, CHEST_LOOT
WEIGHTS: BLOCK_DROP=3.0, CHEST_LOOT=2.0
ZH_NAME: 上好的毒马铃薯
